ERROR: auth failed token=" (rawbin-2026-09-05.log:1726, 5:43PM) — the multipart body arrives EMPTY, 0 bytes; client "Upload failed" = RoomView.ts:101 on null dispatch = server 401. EVIDENCE it's LONG-STANDING not ours: "received 0b" spans ALL 8 daily logs (08-29→09-05) + versions 0.8.169..0.8.175; ZERO successful byte-upload ever logged; 09-04 0b was on 0.8.169/0.8.170 BEFORE any files-surface rework; 0.8.175 touched NO byte/upload/SW code. DISCRIMINATOR: folder-create (fetch+JSON body) SURVIVES this same SW path (works); upload (XHR+FormData multipart STREAM) arrives 0b — the streamed/XHR body does not replay through fetch(event.request). sw.js:102 shows POSTs are not cacheable → intercepting /api POSTs is pure downside. WHO/HOW: US, in CODE (client sw.js), NOT server/proxy config. FIX (small, SW-scoped, SEPARATE loop, not-ours R30.14): SW returns early for method!=="GET" so browser sends /api POSTs natively (body intact; POSTs not cacheable anyway sw.js:102); needs client rebuild+bump+deploy + tester confirm (real drop upload → received >0b). ★ TESTER CONFIRMED (2026-09-05): identical 495KB to REAL prod = intact in 32ms with SERVICE WORKERS BLOCKED → ingress/proxy/size CLEARED (nginx_proxy container maps only 80/443, does NOT front :4444; node direct HTTPS/1.1 own cert, real client IPs). CAUSE = the SW, both code + behaviour. EXACT: sw.js:64 respondWith(navigationStrategy) intercepts ALL fetches (only ws/wss early-returns :66) → :75 all /api/ incl POST → networkFirst :99-101 `await fetch(request)` re-issues the intercepted Request; XHR-multipart streamed body NOT preserved → 0b+empty-token. JSON POSTs (folder-create) survive (small buffered) → the folder-works/upload-fails split. BLAST RADIUS: ALL installed-PWA users with active SW (uploads broken), not Tron-only. FIX = pass non-GET straight through: in the fetch handler `if (event.request.method !== 'GET') return;` (no respondWith) → browser sends /api POSTs natively, body intact; POSTs uncacheable anyway (:102). Needs client rebuild+bump+deploy + tester confirm (SW ACTIVE, real drop → received >0b). ★ FIX BUILT + COMMITTED sha 2fef34dc9 (src/public/sw.js ONLY, path-limited, node --check clean, +6 lines no-restructure): guard `if (event.request.method !== 'GET') return;` after ws/wss check, before respondWith → non-GET /api POSTs go native, body intact. ## ★★ STANDING LAWS (durable-adopted into this anchor so they survive rewind — a pane-message un-adopts) ★★
- **★★★ FUNCTIONAL CONSTRUCTS ARE DEFECTS BY DEFINITION (Tron 2026-09-05, TOP LENS above all)**: a free function / service module / ref→children helper that operates-on or answers-about a TYPE's data is a STRUCTURAL DEFECT the moment written (before any bug) — it puts what belongs to the object elsewhere = a 2nd place truth lives = a duplicate = it diverges. Per-site test = "does this surface ASK AN OBJECT for its OWN children"; import-a-fn-and-pass-a-ref = WRONG no matter how green the guard; the OWNER is the OBJECT (node.children() ON the type), never a module. A shared fetch helper zeroing a one-owner guard = AUTOMATIC FAIL. (An identity registry resolving the ONE shared object per ref is the anti-dup mechanism, distinct from a service that OWNS the answer — architect decides the line, never self.) [[functional-constructs-are-defects-by-definition-the-general-law]]
- **★★★ TRACEABILITY EXISTS TO DEDUPLICATE — it IS the DRY enforcement, not bookkeeping** (Tron 2026-09-05, DEFAULT LENS): one canonical Class = ONE class; one canonical Method = ONE method with ONE Impl; the chain makes duplication impossible/VISIBLE. Two code paths doing the same thing = a TRACEABILITY DEFECT (not a refactor opp). MINT = declare THE ONE canonical thing (a 2nd Impl for a Method = the duplication to stop). GATE = prove there is ONE, not merely that it works. What belongs to the object lives ON the object — DATA (one store, others are symlink views) AND BEHAVIOUR (Folder.children(), surfaces ask the object; no external service). DRY-everywhere / no-dup-index / Folder-owns-children = ONE law. [[traceability-exists-to-deduplicate-it-is-the-dry-enforcement]] [[object-owns-its-answer-dont-pull-it-into-external-machinery]]
- **PRE-BUILD TREE-NOT-REVERTED GATE** (PO 2026-09-02, permanent): before ANY build/restart/deploy run BOTH — (1) VERSION agree: working-tree package.json == committed HEAD == live served; (2) CONTENT agree (the one that matters — version-alone is a FALSE GREEN): grep a recent-shipped symbol, count must match HEAD (resolveDirRefAbs → **2×** in src/ts/server/server.ts; reDeriveDirectChildren → **3×** in src/public/ts/trace/rb-trace-tree.ts). A rewind rolls the WORKING TREE backward WITHOUT touching committed history → today the version was restored while source was still reverted = a build would ship OLD code under a CORRECT version, silently. If either check fails → do NOT build, REPORT; DIRECTION discriminates (deletion-heavy vs HEAD = revert damage → preserve-then-restore from HEAD; insertion-heavy = real WIP → NEVER restore away). After ANY fleet rewind, `git status` EVERY tree, not just the session repo. [[verify-tree-not-rolled-back-before-build]]
- **DEV-MODE = NO UNASKED SECURITY** (Tron 3×, PO 2026-09-02): never design a guard/gate/isolation/confinement/credential into a FEATURE unless Tron asked BY NAME; genuine danger → ONE line to PO + keep building; correctness (both-or-neither) ≠ security. [[dev-mode-no-unasked-security]] · sibling [[security-work-needs-tron-explicit-auth]]

- **★★ DURABLE LESSON: `npm start` BUILDS — there is NO build-free restart via npm start.** start.mjs:71 runs `node build.mjs` (regenerates dist/package.json/sw.js/manifest). deploy.mjs's restart STEP is `C-c + npm start` — I measured that step was build-free but MISSED that npm start itself builds. - **LESSON — deploy.mjs version-ordering:** deploy.mjs reads committedVersion() from package.json ONCE at start, BEFORE its own build stamps package.json from the Config unit. A config-unit-only bump WITHOUT a pre-build → deploy.mjs polls the STALE target (old version) while the restarted server serves the NEW version → false 'served != committed' abort though the deploy SUCCEEDED. CORRECT FLOW: bump Config unit → `npm run build` → commit package.json/sw.js/dist (so package.json == target) → THEN deploy.mjs. (Server WAS restarted + serves the new version; only the poll target was stale.) [[version-bump-mandatory-on-client-fix]]

## ★ MY LEARNINGS (Phase-1 — mine, banked here so they survive the shed)
1. **Validate the instrument before the reading.** A broken tool and a failing subject look identical. THREE catches this week: (a) a 45s "boot defect" was a broken TLS probe — node global `fetch` rejects self-signed certs + ignores NODE_TLS_REJECT_UNAUTHORIZED; use `node:https {rejectUnauthorized:false}`; boot was ~1.3s. (b) DnD "no dataTransfer" was a grep-miss — code aliased `dt=e.dataTransfer`; re-verify a NEGATIVE. (c) server.ts "line#s shifted / a peer is editing" was MY lint collapsing multi-line comments in scanCode; server.ts was stable. Re-verify negatives/surprises by a DIFFERENT method.
2. **A create must ALSO emit — the seam is mutate-only, that's not enough.** UnitController.apply is mutate-existing (throws if absent). 9 of the bypassers CREATE units; declaring them exempt would have gutted the realtime fix (a new unit must appear live). My argument → the architect's `create()` distinct-primitive ruling. Persist and notify must be inseparable (shared `_write`).
3. **Measure differently to verify; an estimate is not a measurement.** I verified "no single choke-point" by counting BYPASSERS of the controller, not re-counting notify-sites (independent lens). The lint then MEASURED 43 sites, not the estimated ~15 — lint before you trust an estimate. Convergence isn't corroboration unless ≥1 side measured the quantity.
4. **Classify then declare; burden of proof on the exception.** 27 exceptions each need a MEASURED reason (not a category wave-through) + tripwires; anything you can't justify defaults to route-through. A too-generous allow-list is how a binding lint stops binding.
5. **Pause at a clean boundary rather than half-do a high-blast-radius pass.** Diligence over urgency. A wall MID a 16-site INV-T-per-site refactor is the worst outcome; a foundation+decided-list handoff is lossless. (This shed decision itself.)
